func gracefulShutdown(apiServer *http.Server, done chan bool) {

                		
            

            
            

                41
                
                    
        // Create context that listens for the interrupt signal from the OS.

                		
            

            
            

                42
                
                    
        ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)

                		
            

            
            

                43
                
                    
        defer stop()

                		
            

            
            

                44
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                45
                
                    
        // Listen for the interrupt signal.

                		
            

            
            

                46
                
                    
        <-ctx.Done()

                		
            

            
            

                47
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                48
                
                    
        log.Println("shutting down gracefully, press Ctrl+C again to force")

                		
            

            
            

                49
                
                    
        stop() // Allow Ctrl+C to force shutdown

                		
            

            
            

                50
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                51
                
                    
        // The context is used to inform the server it has 5 seconds to finish

                		
            

            
            

                52
                
                    
        // the request it is currently handling

                		
            

            
            

                53
                
                    
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)

                		
            

            
            

                54
                
                    
        defer cancel()

                		
            

            
            

                55
                
                    
        if err := apiServer.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                56
                
                    
                log.Printf("Server forced to shutdown with error: %v", err)

                		
            

            
            

                57
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                58
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                59
                
                    
        log.Println("Server exiting")

                		
            

            
            

                60
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                61
                
                    
        // Notify the main goroutine that the shutdown is complete

                		
            

            
            

                62
                
                    
        done <- true

                		
            

            
            

                63
                
                    
}

func (h *AuthHandler) Logout(c *gin.Context) {

                		
            

            
            

                133
                
                    
        authHeader := c.GetHeader("Authorization")

                		
            

            
            

                134
                
                    
        if authHeader == "" {

                		
            

            
            

                135
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authorization header is required")

                		
            

            
            

                136
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                137
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                138
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                139
                
                    
        parts := strings.Split(authHeader, " ")

                		
            

            
            

                140
                
                    
        if len(parts) != 2 || strings.ToLower(parts[0]) != "bearer" {

                		
            

            
            

                141
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Invalid token format")

                		
            

            
            

                142
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                143
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                144
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                145
                
                    
        tokenStr := parts[1]

                		
            

            
            

                146
                
                    
        err := h.authService.Logout(c.Request.Context(), tokenStr)

                		
            

            
            

                147
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                148
                
                    
                MapError(c, err)

                		
            

            
            

                149
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                150
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                151
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                152
                
                    
        api.Success(c, nil)

                		
            

            
            

                153
                
                    
}

func MapError(c *gin.Context, err error) {

                		
            

            
            

                18
                
                    
        if err == nil {

                		
            

            
            

                19
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                20
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                21
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                22
                
                    
        // Handle validation errors that might bubble up from the service layer

                		
            

            
            

                23
                
                    
        verrs := api.FormatValidationError(err)

                		
            

            
            

                24
                
                    
        if verrs != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                25
                
                    
                api.ValidationError(c, verrs)

                		
            

            
            

                26
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                27
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                28
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                29
                
                    
        switch {

                		
            

            
            

                30
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainAuth.ErrUserAlreadyExists):

                		
            

            
            

                31
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusConflict, "USER_EXISTS", "User already exists")

                		
            

            
            

                32
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, appAuth.ErrInvalidEmail):

                		
            

            
            

                33
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "INVALID_EMAIL", "Invalid email address format")

                		
            

            
            

                34
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, appAuth.ErrInvalidRole):

                		
            

            
            

                35
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "INVALID_ROLE", "Invalid user role provided")

                		
            

            
            

                36
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, appAuth.ErrPasswordTooLong):

                		
            

            
            

                37
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "PASSWORD_TOO_LONG", "Password is too long (max 72 characters)")

                		
            

            
            

                38
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainAuth.ErrUserNotFound):

                		
            

            
            

                39
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusNotFound, "USER_NOT_FOUND", "User not found")

                		
            

            
            

                40
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, appAuth.ErrInvalidCredentials):

                		
            

            
            

                41
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "INVALID_CREDENTIALS", "Invalid email or password")

                		
            

            
            

                42
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, appAuth.ErrInvalidRefreshToken):

                		
            

            
            

                43
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN", "Invalid or expired refresh token")

                		
            

            
            

                44
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrRoomNotFound):

                		
            

            
            

                45
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusNotFound, "ROOM_NOT_FOUND", "Room not found")

                		
            

            
            

                46
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrRoomNumberExists):

                		
            

            
            

                47
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusConflict, "ROOM_EXISTS", "Room number already exists")

                		
            

            
            

                48
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainBooking.ErrBookingNotFound):

                		
            

            
            

                49
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusNotFound, "BOOKING_NOT_FOUND", "Booking not found")

                		
            

            
            

                50
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainBooking.ErrBookingConflict):

                		
            

            
            

                51
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusConflict, "BOOKING_CONFLICT", "Booking conflict")

                		
            

            
            

                52
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainBooking.ErrRoomNotAvailable):

                		
            

            
            

                53
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusConflict, "ROOM_NOT_AVAILABLE", "Room is not available for the selected dates")

                		
            

            
            

                54
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainBooking.ErrInvalidBookingDates):

                		
            

            
            

                55
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "INVALID_DATES", "Invalid booking dates")

                		
            

            
            

                56
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainBooking.ErrUnauthorized):

                		
            

            
            

                57
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusForbidden, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Unauthorized to access this booking")

                		
            

            
            

                58
                
                    
        case errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrInvalidRoomStatus),

                		
            

            
            

                59
                
                    
                errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrInvalidDateRange),

                		
            

            
            

                60
                
                    
                errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrPastDate),

                		
            

            
            

                61
                
                    
                errors.Is(err, domainRoom.ErrMissingDates):

                		
            

            
            

                62
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "VALIDATION_ERROR", err.Error())

                		
            

            
            

                63
                
                    
        default:

                		
            

            
            

                64
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusInternalServerError, "SERVER_ERROR", "Internal server error")

                		
            

            
            

                65
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                66
                
                    
}

        id, err := strconv.ParseInt(idStr, 10, 32)

                		
            

            
            

                126
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                127
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "INVALID_ID", "Invalid booking ID")

                		
            

            
            

                128
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                129
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                130
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                131
                
                    
        identityVal, exists := c.Get(middleware.UserContextKey)

                		
            

            
            

                132
                
                    
        if !exists {

                		
            

            
            

                133
                
                    
                api.Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "UNAUTHORIZED", "User not authenticated")

                		
            

            
            

                134
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                135
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                136
                
                    
        claims := identityVal.(*pkgAuth.UserClaims)

                		
            

            
            

                137
                
                    
        isAdmin := claims.Role == string(auth.RoleAdmin) || claims.Role == string(auth.RoleOfficer)

                		
            

            
            

                138
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                139
                
                    
        cancelled, err := h.service.CancelBooking(c.Request.Context(), claims.UserID, isAdmin, int32(id))

                		
            

            
            

                140
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                141
                
                    
                MapError(c, err)

                		
            

            
            

                142
                
                    
                return

                		
            

            
            

                143
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                144
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                145
                
                    
        api.Success(c, dto.ToBookingResponse(cancelled))

                		
            

            
            

                146
                
                    
}

func (s *service) Register(ctx context.Context, email, password string, role domain.UserRole) (*domain.User, error) {

                49

                61

                64
                
                    
                        for _, fe := range ve {

                		
            

            
            

                65
                
                    
                                switch fe.Field() {

                		
            

            
            

                66

                72

func (s *service) RefreshToken(ctx context.Context, refreshToken string) (string, string, error) {

                		
            

            
            

                152
                
                    
        claims, err := s.tokenManager.ValidateRefreshToken(refreshToken)

                		
            

            
            

                153
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                154
                
                    
                slog.WarnContext(ctx, "invalid refresh token attempt", "err", err)

                		
            

            
            

                155
                
                    
                return "", "", ErrInvalidRefreshToken

                		
            

            
            

                156
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                157
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                158
                
                    
        // Check if refresh token is revoked

                		
            

            
            

                159
                
                    
        revoked, err := s.userRepo.IsTokenRevoked(ctx, claims.ID)

                		
            

            
            

                160
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                161
                
                    
                slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to check if refresh token is revoked", "jti", claims.ID, "err", err)

                		
            

            
            

                162
                
                    
                return "", "", fmt.Errorf("failed to check revocation: %w", err)

                		
            

            
            

                163
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                164
                
                    
        if revoked {

                		
            

            
            

                165
                
                    
                slog.WarnContext(ctx, "attempted use of revoked refresh token", "jti", claims.ID)

                		
            

            
            

                166
                
                    
                return "", "", ErrInvalidRefreshToken

                		
            

            
            

                167
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                168
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                169
                
                    
        userID64, err := strconv.ParseInt(claims.Subject, 10, 32)

                		
            

            
            

                170
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                171
                
                    
                return "", "", ErrInvalidRefreshToken

                		
            

            
            

                172
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                173
                
                    
        userID := int32(userID64)

                		
            

            
            

                174
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                175
                
                    
        // Fetch user to get current email and role

                		
            

            
            

                176
                
                    
        user, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(ctx, userID)

                		
            

            
            

                177
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                178
                
                    
                if errors.Is(err, domain.ErrUserNotFound) {

                		
            

            
            

                179
                
                    
                        slog.WarnContext(ctx, "refresh token user not found", "user_id", userID)

                		
            

            
            

                180
                
                    
                        return "", "", ErrInvalidRefreshToken

                		
            

            
            

                181
                
                    
                }

                		
            

            
            

                182
                
                    
                slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to get user for token refresh", "user_id", userID, "err", err)

                		
            

            
            

                183
                
                    
                return "", "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get user: %w", err)

                		
            

            
            

                184
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                185
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                186
                
                    
        newAccessToken, err := s.tokenManager.GenerateToken(user.ID, user.Email, string(user.Role))

                		
            

            
            

                187
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                188
                
                    
                slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to generate new access token", "user_id", user.ID, "err", err)

                190
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                191
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                192
                
                    
        newRefreshToken, err := s.tokenManager.GenerateRefreshToken(user.ID)

                		
            

            
            

                193
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                194
                
                    
                slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to generate new refresh token", "user_id", user.ID, "err", err)

                196
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                197
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                198
                
                    
        // Revoke the old refresh token to prevent reuse (rolling refresh strategy)

                		
            

            
            

                199
                
                    
        if err := s.userRepo.RevokeToken(ctx, claims.ID, claims.ExpiresAt.Time); err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                200
                
                    
                slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to revoke old refresh token during refresh", "jti", claims.ID, "err", err)

                		
            

            
            

                201
                
                    
                // We don't return error here because the new tokens are already generated and valid

                		
            

            
            

                202
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                203
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                204
                
                    
        slog.InfoContext(ctx, "token refreshed successfully", "user_id", user.ID, "old_jti", claims.ID)

                		
            

            
            

                205
                
                    
        return newAccessToken, newRefreshToken, nil

                		
            

            
            

                206
                
                    
}

func (s *Service) SubmitBooking(ctx context.Context, params booking.CreateParams) (*booking.Booking, error) {

                		
            

            
            

                23
                
                    
        // 1. Validate dates - truncate to day for consistency

                		
            

            
            

                24
                
                    
        start := params.StartDate.UTC().Truncate(24 * time.Hour)

                		
            

            
            

                25
                
                    
        end := params.EndDate.UTC().Truncate(24 * time.Hour)

                		
            

            
            

                26
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                27
                
                    
        if start.IsZero() || end.IsZero() {

                		
            

            
            

                28
                
                    
                return nil, booking.ErrInvalidBookingDates

                		
            

            
            

                29
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                30
                
                    
        if !start.Before(end) {

                		
            

            
            

                31
                
                    
                return nil, booking.ErrInvalidBookingDates

                		
            

            
            

                32
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                33
                
                    
        today := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(24 * time.Hour)

                		
            

            
            

                34
                
                    
        if start.Before(today) {

                		
            

            
            

                35
                
                    
                return nil, booking.ErrInvalidBookingDates

                		
            

            
            

                36
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                37
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                38
                
                    
        // 2. Check room exists and get price

                		
            

            
            

                39
                
                    
        rm, err := s.roomRepo.GetByID(ctx, params.RoomID)

                		
            

            
            

                40
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                41
                
                    
                return nil, err

                		
            

            
            

                42
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                43
                
                    
        if rm == nil {

                		
            

            
            

                44
                
                    
                return nil, room.ErrRoomNotFound

                		
            

            
            

                45
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                46
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                47
                
                    
        // 3. Check availability

                		
            

            
            

                48
                
                    
        availableRooms, err := s.roomRepo.ListAvailable(ctx, room.AvailabilityFilter{

                		
            

            
            

                49
                
                    
                StartDate: start,

                		
            

            
            

                50
                
                    
                EndDate:   end,

                		
            

            
            

                51
                
                    
        })

                		
            

            
            

                52
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                53
                
                    
                return nil, err

                		
            

            
            

                54
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                55
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                56
                
                    
        isAvailable := false

                		
            

            
            

                57
                
                    
        for _, ar := range availableRooms {

                		
            

            
            

                58
                
                    
                if ar.ID == params.RoomID {

                		
            

            
            

                59
                
                    
                        isAvailable = true

                		
            

            
            

                60
                
                    
                        break

                		
            

            
            

                61
                
                    
                }

                		
            

            
            

                62
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                63
                
                    
        if !isAvailable {

                		
            

            
            

                64
                
                    
                return nil, booking.ErrRoomNotAvailable

                		
            

            
            

                65
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                66
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                67
                
                    
        // 4. Calculate total price based on nights

                		
            

            
            

                68
                
                    
        nights := int64(end.Sub(start).Hours() / 24)

                		
            

            
            

                69
                
                    
        if nights <= 0 {

                		
            

            
            

                70
                
                    
                nights = 1

                		
            

            
            

                71
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                72

                73
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                74
                
                    
        // 5. Create booking

                		
            

            
            

                75
                
                    
        b := &booking.Booking{

                		
            

            
            

                76
                
                    
                UserID:     params.UserID,

                		
            

            
            

                77
                
                    
                RoomID:     params.RoomID,

                		
            

            
            

                78
                
                    
                StartDate:  start,

                		
            

            
            

                79
                
                    
                EndDate:    end,

                		
            

            
            

                80
                
                    
                TotalPrice: totalPrice,

                		
            

            
            

                81
                
                    
                Status:     booking.StatusConfirmed,

                		
            

            
            

                82
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                83
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                84

                85

func (s *Service) CancelBooking(ctx context.Context, userID int32, isAdmin bool, bookingID int32) (*booking.Booking, error) {

func (s *Service) ListAvailableRooms(ctx context.Context, filter room.AvailabilityFilter) ([]*room.Room, error) {

                		
            

            
            

                58

                59

                60
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                61

                62

                63

                64

                65

                66
                
                    
        if filter.StartDate.UTC().Before(today) {

                		
            

            
            

                67

                68

                69
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                70

                72

                77

                81

                82

                        rooms, err := p.Search(searchCtx, filter)

                		
            

            
            

                84
                
                    
                        select {

                		
            

            
            

                85
                
                    
                        case resChan <- result{rooms, err}:

                        }

                89

                90
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                91

                92

                93
                
                    
                select {

                95

                96

                case <-searchCtx.Done():

                		
            

            
            

                102
                
                    
                        // Timeout or parent context cancelled

                		
            

            
            

                103
                
                    
                        break

                		
            

            
            

                104

                105

                107

func (s *service) Health() map[string]string {

                		
            

            
            

                74

                75
                
                    
        defer cancel()

                77
                
                    
        stats := make(map[string]string)

                		
            

            
            

                78

                80

                81
                
                    
        if err != nil {

                		
            

            
            

                82
                
                    
                stats["status"] = "down"

                		
            

            
            

                83
                
                    
                stats["error"] = fmt.Sprintf("db down: %v", err)

                		
            

            
            

                84
                
                    
                return stats

                		
            

            
            

                85
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                86
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                87
                
                    
        // Database is up, add more statistics

                		
            

            
            

                88
                
                    
        stats["status"] = "up"

                		
            

            
            

                89
                
                    
        stats["message"] = "It's healthy"

                		
            

            
            

                90
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                91
                
                    
        // Get database stats (like open connections, in use, idle, etc.)

                		
            

            
            

                92
                
                    
        dbStats := s.db.Stats()

                		
            

            
            

                93
                
                    
        stats["open_connections"] = strconv.Itoa(dbStats.OpenConnections)

                		
            

            
            

                94
                
                    
        stats["in_use"] = strconv.Itoa(dbStats.InUse)

                		
            

            
            

                95
                
                    
        stats["idle"] = strconv.Itoa(dbStats.Idle)

                		
            

            
            

                96
                
                    
        stats["wait_count"] = strconv.FormatInt(dbStats.WaitCount, 10)

                		
            

            
            

                97
                
                    
        stats["wait_duration"] = dbStats.WaitDuration.String()

                		
            

            
            

                98
                
                    
        stats["max_idle_closed"] = strconv.FormatInt(dbStats.MaxIdleClosed, 10)

                		
            

            
            

                99

                100
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                101
                
                    
        // Evaluate stats to provide a health message

                		
            

            
            

                102
                
                    
        if dbStats.OpenConnections > 40 { // Assuming 50 is the max for this example

                		
            

            
            

                103
                
                    
                stats["message"] = "The database is experiencing heavy load."

                		
            

            
            

                104
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                105
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                106
                
                    
        if dbStats.WaitCount > 1000 {

                		
            

            
            

                107
                
                    
                stats["message"] = "The database has a high number of wait events, indicating potential bottlenecks."

                		
            

            
            

                108
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                109
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                110
                
                    
        if dbStats.MaxIdleClosed > int64(dbStats.OpenConnections)/2 {

                		
            

            
            

                111
                
                    
                stats["message"] = "Many idle connections are being closed, consider revising the connection pool settings."

                		
            

            
            

                112
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                113
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                114
                
                    
        if dbStats.MaxLifetimeClosed > int64(dbStats.OpenConnections)/2 {

                		
            

            
            

                115
                
                    
                stats["message"] = "Many connections are being closed due to max lifetime, consider increasing max lifetime or revising the connection usage pattern."

                		
            

            
            

                116
                
                    
        }

                		
            

            
            

                117
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                118

func (r *SQLCRoomRepository) GetDetail(ctx context.Context, id int32) (*room.Detail, error) {

                		
            

            
            

                156

                160
                
                    

                		
            

            
            

                161

                162

                163

                164
